Valley Corporation">Damodar Valley Corporation</a></td></tr></tbody></table>

<p id="mwBg">The <b id="mwBw">Maithon Dam</b> is located at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maithon" title="Maithon" id="mwCA">Maithon</a>, 48<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwCQ"> </span>km from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dhanbad" title="Dhanbad" id="mwCg">Dhanbad</a>, in the state of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jharkhand" title="Jharkhand" id="mwCw">Jharkhand</a> India.It is just 24<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwDA"> </span>km from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asansol" title="Asansol" id="mwDQ">Asansol</a> city and one of the topmost visited place in Asansol region.
<span about="#mwt11" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwDg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"15712"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>15,712</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt11"> </span><span about="#mwt11">ft (4,789</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt11"> </span><span about="#mwt11">m)</span> long and <span about="#mwt12" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwDw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"165"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>165</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt12"> </span><span about="#mwt12">ft (50</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt12"> </span><span about="#mwt12">m)</span> high.<sup about="#mwt15" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-govt_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"govt"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-govt-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-govt-1" id="mwEA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwEQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwFA">Maithon Dam is 194 km away from Ranchi. It is one of the biggest dams in Jharkhand. This dam is built on the Barakar River. The underground power station present here is the main center of attraction for tourists. Maithon Dam is known for its natural environment and religious importance.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Wikipedia_articles_needing_clarification_from_September_2025" about="#mwt16" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwFQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Clarify","href":"./Template:Clarify"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"},"reason":{"wt":"How does a concrete dam, built in the 1950s, have \"religious importance\"? The body of the article makes no mention of religious importance? What supports this editorial?"}},"i":0}}]}'/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt16" id="mwFg"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Please_clarify" title="Wikipedia:Please clarify"><span title='How does a concrete dam, built in the 1950s, have "religious importance"? The body of the article makes no mention of religious importance? What supports this editorial? (September 2025)'>clarification needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup><sup about="#mwt19" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwFw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGQ">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwGw">This dam was specially designed for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flood_control" title="Flood control" class="mw-redirect" id="mwHA">flood control</a> and generates 60,000<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwHQ"> </span>kW of electric power. There is an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Underground_power_station" title="Underground power station" id="mwHg">underground power station</a>, the first of its kind in the whole of South East Asia.  The dam is constructed on the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barakar_River" title="Barakar River" id="mwHw">Barakar River</a>. The lake is spread over <span about="#mwt20" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwIA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"65"},"2":{"wt":"km2"},"3":{"wt":"mi2"}},"i":0}}]}'>65 square kilometres (25</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt20"> </span><span about="#mwt20">mi</span><sup about="#mwt20">2</sup><span about="#mwt20">)</span>.<sup about="#mwt21" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-govt_1-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"govt"}}'><a href="#cite_note-govt-1" id="mwIQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

Urban centre, D: Dam, P: Power Plant<br/>Owing to space constraints in the small map, the actual locations in a larger map may vary slightly</div></div></div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt22"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_OSM_location_maps"/></span>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwLw"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Damodar_Map.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwMA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Damodar_Map.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c9/Damodar_Map.jpg/250px-Damodar_Map.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c9/Damodar_Map.jpg/500px-Damodar_Map.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1753" data-file-height="1366" data-file-type="bitmap" height="195" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwMQ"/></a><figcaption id="mwMg">Damodar Basin</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwMw"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwNA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg/250px-Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg/500px-Foundation_stone_of_maithon_dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="4608" data-file-height="3072" data-file-type="bitmap" height="167" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwNQ"/></a><figcaption id="mwNg">Inauguration plaque of Maithon Dam</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwNw">The chief ministers of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bihar" title="Bihar" id="mwOA">Bihar</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Bengal" title="West Bengal" id="mwOQ">West Bengal</a>, Dr. Srikrishna Sinha and Dr. Bidhan Chandra Roy, persuaded central government about the benefits of this mega project. DVC is the first-ever multipurpose river valley project of independent India it came into being on July 7, 1948, by an act of Constituent Assembly (Act no. XIV of 1948).
The Damodar Valley Corporation (DVC) has its command area of approx 24,235<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwOg"> </span>km<sup id="mwOw">2</sup> spreading across the Damodar basin. The upper valley consists of two entire districts (Dhanbad and Bokaro) and eight districts partly in the state of Jharkhand. The lower valley on the other hand consists of five districts partly (Burdwan, Hooghly, Bankura, Howrah and Purulia) in the state of West Bengal.</p>

<p id="mwPA">DVC was set up with the intent of promoting and operating the schemes which may cause social and economic uplift in the valley region.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt25" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwPQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Citation needed","href":"./Template:Citation_needed"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"March 2011"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_March_2011" about="#mwt25"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt25" id="mwPg"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (March 2011)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwPw"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maithon_Dam.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwQA"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maithon_Dam.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e5/Maithon_Dam.jpg/250px-Maithon_Dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e5/Maithon_Dam.jpg/500px-Maithon_Dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="4731" data-file-height="3070" data-file-type="bitmap" height="162" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwQQ"/></a><figcaption id="mwQg">Sunset at Maithon dam</figcaption></figure>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwQw" aria-labelledby="Prospects_and_opportunities"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Prospects_and_opportunities">Prospects and opportunities</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Prospects and opportunities"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwRA">Proficient management of water resources through dams, canals and barage, DVC facilitated irrigation as well as industrial and domestic water supply which at large benefited the region as a whole.
DVC has been generating, transmitting &amp; distributing electrical energy since 1953 and has succeeded not only in meeting the expectation of consumers but has also elevated its height in its performance. The full form of DVC is Damodar valley corporation and since it uses its main source of water from the Damodar dam it is named so.</p>

<p id="mwRQ">Recently it has collaborated with TATA Power and a thermal power project has been started, named Maithon Power Limited (MPL).
Earlier DVC had installed few Gas Turbines for generation of electricity, a unique technology in the entire region introduced for the very first time in Maithon. They are no more functional now.</p>

<p id="mwRg">DVC has provided employment to thousands of families and is continuing to do so. It also provides facility for engineering and management graduates to undergo training in their respective fields.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt26" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwRw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt26"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt26" id="mwSA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwSQ" aria-labelledby="Maithon"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Maithon">Maithon</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Maithon"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwSg">48<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwSw"> </span>km from Dhanbad, Maithon is termed as mother's abode. It has the biggest reservoir in DVC. It has been designed for flood control &amp; generates 60,000<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwTA"> </span>kW of electric power. It is built on the river Barakar.</p>

<p id="mwTQ">It has its unique underground power station, the first of its kind in South Asia. The lake is spread over an area of 65<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwTg"> </span>km<sup id="mwTw">2</sup>. It is <span about="#mwt27" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwUA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"15712"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>15,712</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt27"> </span><span about="#mwt27">ft (4,789</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt27"> </span><span about="#mwt27">m)</span>. long &amp; <span about="#mwt28" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwUQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"165"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>165</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt28"> </span><span about="#mwt28">ft (50</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt28"> </span><span about="#mwt28">m)</span> high.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt29" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwUg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt29"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt29" id="mwUw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwVA">The dam's mission comprises:</p>
<dl id="mwVQ"><dd id="mwVg">flood Control</dd>
<dd id="mwVw">promotion &amp; operation of schemes for irrigation</dd>
<dd id="mwWA">water supply for domestic &amp; industrial use</dd>
<dd id="mwWQ">navigation &amp; drainage</dd>
<dd id="mwWg">generation, transmission &amp; distribution of electrical energy.</dd></dl>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwWw" aria-labelledby="History"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="History">History</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: History"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwXA">In 1942, during the course of World War II, a flood occurred in the Damodar valley region that cut Calcutta off from the rest of the country for a period of 10 weeks. Soon the government sprang into action &amp; called Mr. W.L.Voord, a civil engineer of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tennessee_Valley_Authority" title="Tennessee Valley Authority" id="mwXQ">Tennessee Valley Authority</a> (TVA). He designed the plan of the dams by 1945. Finally the first dam of independent India came up on July 7, 1953, at Tilaiya. Soon Konar, Maithon &amp; Panchet Dam followed in its footsteps in 1955, 1957 and 1959 respectively.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt30" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwXg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt30"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt30" id="mwXw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwYA" aria-labelledby="Operation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Operation">Operation</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Operation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwYQ">Damodar river is a seasonal river, basically 82% of which is filled up by rainfall in July–September. 1 June to 31 October is the filling period during which rainwater is stored and the stored rainwater is used for irrigation by the downstream areas of Burdwan, hydroelectric power generation and scores of other activities.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt31" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwYg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt31"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt31" id="mwYw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mwZA" aria-labelledby="Maithon_Hydel"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Maithon_Hydel">Maithon Hydel</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Maithon Hydel"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwZQ">Maithon Hydel Power Station (MHPS) is located on the river Barakar about 12.9<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwZg"> </span>km above its confluence with the Damodar near the border of Dhanbad &amp; Burdwan districts of the states of Jharkhand &amp; West Bengal respectively. The unique feature of this is that it is located underground in the left bank of the river and is the first underground hydel power station of South East Asia. The power station has a total generating capacity of 60 MW with 3 units of 20 MW each. MHPS is located at almost 100 feet below the water level, and is the first of its kind, in South East Asia. After RM&amp;U of unit no 2 of MHPS, its capacity was enhanced and the total Installed capacity of the plant is now 63.2 MW.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt32" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwZw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt32"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt32" id="mwaA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwaQ" aria-labelledby="Reservoir_operation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Reservoir_operation">Reservoir operation</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Reservoir operation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwag"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maithon_dam.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwaw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maithon_dam.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/af/Maithon_dam.jpg/250px-Maithon_dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/af/Maithon_dam.jpg/500px-Maithon_dam.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="2560" data-file-height="1920" data-file-type="bitmap" height="188" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwbA"/></a><figcaption id="mwbQ">Reservoir of Maithon</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwbg">A vast hydro meteorological station with VHF/wireless facilities exists over the Damodar catchment area. On a real time basis (hourly, 3 hourly, 6 hourly) data of river gauge, rainfall river discharge are measured and transmitted through existing communication network to the central flood station at Maithon. The Indian Meteorological Department, Kolkata, transmits the rainfall forecast for the next 24 hours for the entire Damodar Valley region to Maithon. A computer model at the Flood Forecasting Unit at Maithon estimates the inflow into the reservoirs based on the real time data and rainfall forecast.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt33" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwbw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt33"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt33" id="mwcA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwcQ">According to detailed guidelines contained in the "Damodar Valley Reservoir Regulation Manual", Member Secretary of DVRRC (Damodar Valley Reservoir Regulation Committee) after discussion with SE, Damodar Irrigation Circle Govt. of West Bengal, Reservoir Operation of DVC &amp; SE, Tenughat of Govt. of Jharkhand decides day to day release of water from Maithon Dam and Panchet Dam, and issues advice for release of water through the dams.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt34" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwcg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt34"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt34" id="mwcw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwdA" aria-labelledby="Problems"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Problems">Problems</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Problems"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwdQ">People involved in power generation say that water should be filled up as much as possible as it would be better for power generation. People involved in flood control department says water storage should be lowered, so that in case of excessive rain a flood like situation arises then it will be a flood cushion. This is a conflicting nature, so water has to be kept at an optimum level to satisfy both parties.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt35" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwdg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt35"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt35" id="mwdw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mweA">Dead storage capacity of the dam during the initial years was much more than the storage capacity now. The portion of a water storage's capacity that is equal to the volume of water below the level of the lowest outlet (the minimum supply level), is known as th Dead Storage Capacity. This, increase has been mainly due to the sedimentation storage. Due to human &amp; industrial activities, the storage capacity has decreased over the years.
As per the design of dam, capacity depth should have been <span about="#mwt36" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mweQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"500"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>500</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt36"> </span><span about="#mwt36">ft (150</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt36"> </span><span about="#mwt36">m)</span>. But due to non-acquisition of land the depth is at <span about="#mwt37" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mweg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"495"},"2":{"wt":"ft"},"3":{"wt":"m"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>495</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt37"> </span><span about="#mwt37">ft (151</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt37"> </span><span about="#mwt37">m)</span>.
The carrying capacity of the dam was <span about="#mwt38" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwew" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"1000000"},"2":{"wt":"cuft/s"},"3":{"wt":"m3/s"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>1,000,000</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt38"> </span><span about="#mwt38">cu</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt38"> </span><span about="#mwt38">ft/s (28,000</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt38"> </span><span about="#mwt38">m</span><sup about="#mwt38">3</sup><span about="#mwt38">/s)</span>. But at the time of construction it was only <span about="#mwt39" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwfA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"250000"},"2":{"wt":"cuft/s"},"3":{"wt":"m3/s"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>250,000</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt39"> </span><span about="#mwt39">cu</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt39"> </span><span about="#mwt39">ft/s (7,100</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt39"> </span><span about="#mwt39">m</span><sup about="#mwt39">3</sup><span about="#mwt39">/s)</span> and at present it has a carrying capacity of less than <span about="#mwt40" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwfQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"100000"},"2":{"wt":"cuft/s"},"3":{"wt":"m3/s"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>100,000</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt40"> </span><span about="#mwt40">cu</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt40"> </span><span about="#mwt40">ft/s (2,800</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt40"> </span><span about="#mwt40">m</span><sup about="#mwt40">3</sup><span about="#mwt40">/s)</span>. The cost of removing deposited silt in the dam is equal to the construction of a new dam, therefore economically not viable. If the silt is removed and stored in the banks of the dam, then during monsoon, the silt would get washed off into the dam.</p>

<p id="mwfg">Maithon Dam has completed 60 years in service, and this naturally has an effect on the structures, in spite of regular maintenance by DVC.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt41" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwfw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt41"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt41" id="mwgA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwgQ" aria-labelledby="Flood_warning"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Flood_warning">Flood warning</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Flood warning"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwgg">Before releasing the water from dam's spillway gates, a necessary warning is communicated to district authorities, affected downstream industries, municipalities and others including senior administrative officials of Burdwan, Bankura, Purulia; IOW Dept. Govt. of WB; EE, DHW, Durgapur; Member; CWC, New Delhi.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Wikipedia_articles_needing_clarification_from_May_2021" about="#mwt42" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwgw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"clarify","href":"./Template:Clarify"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Explain the abbreviations"},"date":{"wt":"May 2021"}},"i":0}}]}'/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt42" id="mwhA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Please_clarify" title="Wikipedia:Please clarify"><span title="Explain the abbreviations (May 2021)">clarification needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> Whenever there is a discharge from Maithon and Panchet dams exceeding <span about="#mwt43" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwhQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"convert","href":"./Template:Convert"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"1132"},"2":{"wt":"cuft/s"},"3":{"wt":"m3/s"},"abbr":{"wt":"on"}},"i":0}}]}'>1,132</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt43"> </span><span about="#mwt43">cu</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt43"> </span><span about="#mwt43">ft/s (32.1</span><span typeof="mw:Entity" about="#mwt43"> </span><span about="#mwt43">m</span><sup about="#mwt43">3</sup><span about="#mwt43">/s)</span>, special arrangements are broadcast through Doordarshan and AIR regional news bulletins. Doordarshan, Kolkata also telecasts daily weather forecasts with probable rainfall in the Damodar Valley.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt44" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwhg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt44"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt44" id="mwhw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwiA">Maithon Dam has completed its 60 successful years in the service to the nation but, the maintenance of the reservoir is a matter of concern. In these 60 years the depth of the reservoir has decreased, so it gets flooded very soon. So to maintain the safe level the water has to be released through dams. It has a dual impact, first it hits badly to the irrigation in Burdwan District also the power generation capacity MHPS as the capacity of the reservoir has decreased in last 60 years.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt45" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwiQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt45"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt45" id="mwig"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwiw">The rain water takes a lot of soil with it and the same settles down in the reservoir causing decrease in the depth.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt46" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwjA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"fact","href":"./Template:Fact"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"September 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_September_2025" about="#mwt46"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt46" id="mwjQ"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (September 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwjg" aria-labelledby="References"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="References">References</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Maithon_Dam&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: References"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
